## CAND-000001 — Local-first academic operating system

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Core Product
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-01): Binding product identity; current pre-beta implementation is local-first. Evidence summary (C-01): AXOM is a private academic operating system for high-pressure learners; it should remain useful without an account, provider, or network. Intended feeling: the learner owns a dependable system rather than renting access to their own work. Documented product evidence (C-01): Local Vault is primary; cloud capability is optional. Critical studying cannot depend on cloud availability. Observable evidence (C-01): App remains usable without `DATABASE_URL`; local Workspace persists independently of app code; optional remote features are honestly unavailable when not configured. Observed evidence (H-01): repeatedly stated and implemented in the shipped core. Evidence summary (H-01): Design intent is a durable medical-student operating system rather than disconnected tools. Core promise: meaningful work remains usable offline and under the learner's control. Owner feeling: personal, dependable, “on their side,” and easier than manually managing a study system. Observable evidence (H-01): ordinary daily workflows remain useful without cloud; local data survives reload; product truth does not imply unavailable intelligence or integrations.
- **Evidence Sources:** EVID-CAND-000001-001 — sources/core-systems.md:40 (C-01) — `README.md:3-6,23-36,98-99; EVID-CAND-000001-002 — sources/core-systems.md:40 (C-01) — `docs/PRE-ALPHA-CONTRACT.md:53-62; EVID-CAND-000001-003 — sources/core-systems.md:40 (C-01) — `docs/governance/AX-0002-CONSTITUTION.md:47-51; EVID-CAND-000001-004 — sources/core-systems.md:40 (C-01) — `docs/governance/AX-0009-PRODUCT-LEXICON.md:211-225.; EVID-CAND-000001-005 — sources/historical-review.md:55 (H-01) — Pre-Beta directive lines 1–40; EVID-CAND-000001-006 — sources/historical-review.md:55 (H-01) — Wave 5.5D lines 24–63; EVID-CAND-000001-007 — sources/historical-review.md:55 (H-01) — full audit §§1, 5, 17, 20.; EVID-CAND-000001-008 — docs/governance/AX-0009-PRODUCT-LEXICON.md — AX-0009 §2.2

## CAND-000002 — Daily academic operating loop

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Daily Loop
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-02): Implemented product center of gravity; accepted/no-regress surface. Evidence summary (C-02): Central job: “open the app overwhelmed → leave with one clear next move.” Loop: Open → Orient → Commit → Study → Capture → Review → Recover → Return tomorrow. Documented product evidence (C-02): AXOM is not a widget collection; each visit should reduce triage and deposit evidence that improves the next visit. Observable evidence (C-02): Before studying, one decision; during studying, reliable session and capture; after studying, a bounded closeout that seeds tomorrow.

## CAND-000004 — Explainable, evidence-identical recommendations

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Cross-product; AI/Product Truth
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-04): Binding no-black-box rule; implemented in Command Brief. Evidence summary (C-04): Trust compounds when the learner can see why. Complexity belongs inside the system, but consequential logic must remain inspectable. Documented product evidence (C-04): Every major recommendation exposes the exact evidence/contribution rows used, not a reconstructed rationale. Observable evidence (C-04): Learner can answer why a recommendation, score, lecture, or priority changed; displayed evidence and the decision calculation are identical. Observed evidence (H-04): accepted, core deterministic paths comply. Evidence summary (H-04): Design intent is intelligence that remains inspectable and honest. Product truth: no placeholder intelligence, fake AI, hidden provider call, or unsupported confidence. Core promise: suggestions never silently become canonical user facts. Observable evidence (H-04): provider-free core remains functional; generated or inferred output is labeled, evidence-backed, reviewable, and optional.

## CAND-000005 — Calm recovery without shame or attention competition

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Experience; Habits
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-05): Permanent design philosophy; recovery behavior implemented. Evidence summary (C-05): AXOM quietly organizes attention, reduces uncertainty, and never scolds a learner who is behind. Documented product evidence (C-05): Recovery sorts, shrinks, and restarts work. Streak guilt, red-alert theatrics, and competitive leaderboards do not drive the core loop. Observable evidence (C-05): Recovery language is factual and editable; fresh users are not assigned missed history; leaderboards remain deprioritized/hidden. Observed evidence (H-11): habit surface remains WIP. Evidence summary (H-11): Misses can be partial, intentional skips, rescheduled, or recovery starts; shame-heavy streak mechanics are rejected. Observable evidence (H-11): reminders stop after completion, quiet mode exists, user selects escalation, and copy supports recovery.
- **Evidence Sources:** EVID-CAND-000005-001 — sources/core-systems.md:135 (C-05) — `docs/PRE-ALPHA-CONTRACT.md:64-92; EVID-CAND-000005-002 — sources/core-systems.md:135 (C-05) — `PRODUCT_RESEARCH_AND_OPPORTUNITIES.md:21-23,27-36; EVID-CAND-000005-003 — sources/core-systems.md:135 (C-05) — `FEATURES.md:398-422; EVID-CAND-000005-004 — sources/core-systems.md:135 (C-05) — `ROADMAP.md:82-84.; EVID-CAND-000005-005 — sources/historical-review.md:215 (H-11) — Pre-Beta directive lines 353–439; EVID-CAND-000005-006 — sources/historical-review.md:215 (H-11) — full audit feature matrix `#habits.

## CAND-000006 — Honest capability boundaries

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Cross-product; Release Truth; Shell; Cross-product/Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-06): Accepted and repeated across shipped surfaces. Evidence summary (C-06): Trust over cleverness; a visible unknown is safer than a confident invention. Documented product evidence (C-06): AXOM does not fake OCR, AI, cloud sync, integration, update capability, metrics, or evidence. Locked/deferred surfaces state their real boundary. Observable evidence (C-06): No canned output is labeled AI; unsupported data remains unknown; unavailable integrations are non-actionable and plainly labeled; no invented percentages or seed-derived user claims. Observed evidence (H-16): typed badges implemented, multiple modules remain WIP. Evidence summary (H-16): Planned capability can remain visible as ecosystem context, but cannot pretend to work. Observable evidence (H-16): WIP/BUILDING/Under Construction label, concise limitation, no fake progress or dead action. Observed evidence (H-22): Verified. Evidence summary (H-22): premium AXOM identity plus truthful module state and user-controlled Light/Dark/System appearance. Observable evidence (H-22): accessible disclosure, device-local theme, flash-free paint, cross-tab/OS sync, no schema regression. Observed evidence (H-27): Verified. Evidence summary (H-27): users can understand current product truth and trust imported questions through inspectable provenance. Observable evidence (H-27): mixed keys preserved, no fallback A, real-import verifier, source-only save, truthful onboarding/help/feedback, deterministic Command Brief.
- **Evidence Sources:** EVID-CAND-000006-001 — sources/core-systems.md:159 (C-06) — `README.md:64-78; EVID-CAND-000006-002 — sources/core-systems.md:159 (C-06) — `FEATURES.md:539-568; EVID-CAND-000006-003 — sources/core-systems.md:159 (C-06) — `docs/UPDATE-POLICY.md:8-15; EVID-CAND-000006-004 — sources/core-systems.md:159 (C-06) — `docs/governance/AX-0002-CONSTITUTION.md:55-59; EVID-CAND-000006-005 — sources/core-systems.md:159 (C-06) — `docs/governance/AX-0010-UX-STANDARDS.md:227-241.; EVID-CAND-000006-006 — sources/historical-review.md:288 (H-16) — owner alpha corpus large Alpha release directive, “Under Construction UI Pattern”; EVID-CAND-000006-007 — sources/historical-review.md:288 (H-16) — owner blueprint corpus message 10; EVID-CAND-000006-008 — sources/historical-review.md:288 (H-16) — full audit feature matrix.; EVID-CAND-000006-009 — sources/historical-review.md:379 (H-22) — Wave 2 attachment acceptance summary; EVID-CAND-000006-010 — sources/historical-review.md:379 (H-22) — commit `3a8069b.; EVID-CAND-000006-011 — sources/historical-review.md:439 (H-27) — commit `5c35e77; EVID-CAND-000006-012 — sources/historical-review.md:439 (H-27) — accepted 657 tests and 4 browser journeys in commit body.

## CAND-000008 — Command Brief: one grounded next action

- **Observed Category:** Feature; Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Command Brief; Dashboard; Cross-product/Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-08): Implemented; accepted/no-regress. Evidence summary (C-08): Remove daily triage. Intended user outcome: “I know what to do next and why.” Documented product evidence (C-08): One supported next action, reason, effort, outcome, and smaller fallback; never presented as AI unless AI contributed. Observable evidence (C-08): Deterministic ranking; visible exact evidence; stable tie behavior; explicit user action before any state mutation. Observed evidence (H-19): implemented and verified. Evidence summary (H-19): It ranks one best next action from real user evidence, explains why, and remains neutral while learning. It replaces redundant fake suggested-action surfaces. ## CAND-000016 — Configurable, neutral daily success

- **Observed Category:** Feature; Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Daily Progress; Daily Loop
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-16): Implemented. Evidence summary (C-16): Daily progress should reflect the learner’s own chosen signals without retroactive or default failure. Documented product evidence (C-16): Requirements are optional, scheduled, tracking-start bounded, and neutral when empty. Observable evidence (C-16): One canonical evaluation feeds Productivity, Dashboard, and Reports; pre-tracking/off-schedule days do not fail; no default card/Anki requirement for new profiles. Observed evidence (H-08): implemented and verified in Wave 5.5A/B. Evidence summary (H-08): A successful day reflects the learner's enabled requirements, schedule, and chosen units; the product must not punish someone for a capability they never enabled. Observable evidence (H-08): Anki/cards can be disabled; new requirements create no historical misses; daily/weekday/weekly schedules respect tracking start; neutral state precedes fair evaluation. Observed evidence (H-26): Verified. Evidence summary (H-26): logging takes seconds and success reflects user-selected requirements rather than product assumptions. Observable evidence (H-26): neutral first state, rotating examples, recent/frequent actions, schedule-aware metrics, soft-limit presets, background timer safety.

## CAND-000022 — Grounded learner readiness

- **Observed Category:** Feature; Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Readiness; Wellbeing
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-22): Implemented deterministic foundation; future evolution referenced. Evidence summary (C-22): Capacity may resize a suggestion, never silently rewrite the learner’s work or infer health meaning from unconfirmed language. Documented product evidence (C-22): Readiness is an explainable capacity signal, not proof of learning or a diagnosis. Observable evidence (C-22): Neutral baseline ignored; grounded/user-confirmed evidence only; calculation and threshold visible; user can review, restore, update, or inspect; saved data unchanged by presentation suggestion. Observed evidence (H-10): partially implemented. Evidence summary (H-10): The system may organize self-reported and deterministic signals, but must not diagnose, silently infer conclusions from journal text, or moralize health behavior. Observable evidence (H-10): visible factors/weights, possible signals require confirmation, manual correction is preserved, opt-out/deletion exists.

- **Observed Category:** Feature

## CAND-000062 — Automatic Safety Snapshots and migration recovery

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Data Safety; Onboarding/Settings
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-62): Implemented. Evidence summary (C-62): Risky data changes must have a recoverable local checkpoint, and failure must never look like deletion. Documented product evidence (C-62): Additive migrations create pre-migration safety snapshots; unresolved failure remains visible with export/restore/ retry choices. Observable evidence (C-62): Previous/current versions and snapshot readability visible; original Workspace explicitly preserved; failure cannot be dismissed; state clears only after successful retry. Observed evidence (H-24): Verified. Evidence summary (H-24): setup remains comprehensible, recovery actions are real and non-destructive, and local-first language remains truthful. Observable evidence (H-24): five settings sections, four onboarding steps, seven meaningful tour steps, optional exact-date reminders, schema v32, no workspace localStorage. Observed evidence (H-82): partially implemented. Evidence summary (H-82): JSON expertise must not be the only disaster plan. Observable evidence (H-82): bounded automatic snapshots, visible backup age, reminder after meaningful growth/risky operation, one-click recovery, plaintext warning.

## CAND-000065 — Restore-history audit trail

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Backup
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-65): Proposed. Evidence summary (C-65): Recovery history should be inspectable rather than relying on memory. ## CAND-000067 — Cloud sync/account hardening

- **Observed Category:** Research; Bug
- **Observed Area/System:** Accounts + Sync; Security/Accounts; Accounts/Sync
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-67): Blocked; experimental name-only scaffold retained, no primary product claim. Evidence summary (C-67): Local-first ownership and value before account; no PIN or name-only mechanism may masquerade as secure authentication. Documented product evidence (C-67): Any future remote sync needs real identity, consent, authentication, security review, recovery, and conflict clarity; secrets never live in the client. Observable evidence (C-67): No primary Settings claim while unsafe; local use remains complete; future auth is email magic link/OAuth/ passkey-class, revocable, and security-reviewed. Observed evidence (H-37): Open at audit baseline. Documented product evidence (H-37): legacy load/save paths accept UUID/name without attaching a verified session, exposing private academic/journal/question snapshots if cloud is enabled. Observable evidence (H-37): every data endpoint verifies session and ownership, or cloud controls remain disabled in external builds. Observed evidence (H-71): Beta/Later Evidence summary (H-71): eventual cross-device continuity must not make cloud a prerequisite or let a short PIN masquerade as security. Observable evidence (H-71): unique account identity, verified ownership, OAuth/passkey or equivalent option, recovery, encryption policy, conflict resolution, anonymous-workspace migration, deletion/export.

- **Observed Category:** Research
- **Observed Area/System:** Platform; Distribution/Native
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (C-71): Experimental scaffold; not an Alpha primary channel. ## CAND-000101 — Question Bank as AXOM's flagship practice loop

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-01): Confirmed product direction; the current product contains the corresponding Question Bank system. Evidence summary (Q-01): - **Observed design intent:** Turn messy source material into reliable practice while always making the next useful action obvious. - **Observed product principle:** One cohesive academic operating system; convenience is part of the product. - **Observed core promise:** “Import, review, quiz, repair, repeat.” - **Observed user feeling:** The Question Bank should feel premium, powerful, convenient, and reliable, not like disconnected React screens. - **Observed product truth:** The Question Bank is a flagship product pillar, not an ancillary file viewer. Observable evidence (Q-01): A first-time learner can enter through Import; a returning learner can continue, review mappings, or practice due, missed, and weak material; uncertain mappings remain outside scored pools. Observed evidence (H-05): implemented and verified at the landing/workflow level. Evidence summary (H-05): Design intent is that a new user understands the primary action within about five seconds. Core promise: messy source material becomes reviewable, trusted practice rather than opaque automation. Observable evidence (H-05): one dominant import action, uncertain mappings reviewed, only finalized questions runnable, returning users see continuation and review needs first.

## CAND-000103 — Mapping Readiness is the runnable truth boundary

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Import trust / Quiz eligibility; Question Integrity; Cross-product/Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-03): Verified and lexically canonical. Evidence summary (Q-03): - **Observed design intent:** Prevent uncertain parser output from becoming a false answer presented to the learner. - **Observed product principle:** Trust over cleverness; unresolved beats falsely ready. - **Observed core promise:** Only `Ready` answer mappings are runnable and scoreable as known answers. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM will tell me when it does not know.” - **Observed product truth:** `Ready`, `Review suggested`, and `Unresolved` are different trust states, not cosmetic labels. Observable evidence (Q-03): Unresolved and review-suggested mappings are excluded from runnable pools; false-ready count remains zero; repair changes mapping diagnostics without rewriting attempts. Observed evidence (H-06): implemented and verified for pasted text and CSV/JSON. Evidence summary (H-06): Product truth: a higher unresolved rate is preferable to a wrongly trusted answer. Explicit evidence is preserved; uncertainty is surfaced; no path defaults to A. ## CAND-000104 — Unknown answers never default to A

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Import trust; Question Integrity; Cross-product/Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-04): Verified invariant. Evidence summary (Q-04): - **Observed design intent:** Make absence, invalidity, and conflict visible instead of manufacturing certainty. - **Observed product principle:** Trust over cleverness. - **Observed core promise:** Missing, malformed, ambiguous, or conflicting answer evidence never becomes option A or the first option. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM would rather stop me than confidently teach me the wrong answer.” - **Observed product truth:** Unknown is a valid state; questions-only import is not a parser failure. Observable evidence (Q-04): No default index, invalid labels remain unresolved, conflicts remain conflicts, confirmed keys must exist among options, and `B,D,A,C,E` survives exactly. Observed evidence (H-06): implemented and verified for pasted text and CSV/JSON. Evidence summary (H-06): Product truth: a higher unresolved rate is preferable to a wrongly trusted answer. Explicit evidence is preserved; uncertainty is surfaced; no path defaults to A. Observable evidence (H-06): `falseReady = 0`, mixed keys do not collapse, harmless drift remains a review-gated candidate, contradiction remains unresolved, and user confirmation outranks reparsing. Observed evidence (H-27): Verified. Evidence summary (H-27): users can understand current product truth and trust imported questions through inspectable provenance. Observable evidence (H-27): mixed keys preserved, no fallback A, real-import verifier, source-only save, truthful onboarding/help/feedback, deterministic Command Brief.

## CAND-000105 — User-confirmed answer mappings outrank parser output

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Import trust / Repair; Question Integrity; Cross-product/Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-05): Verified. ## CAND-000106 — Source text and learner overlays remain distinct

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Question provenance / Annotations; Question Annotations
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-06): Verified for notes, highlights, and repair behavior. Evidence summary (Q-06): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve source truth and learner work without making either overwrite the other. - **Observed product principle:** One source of truth; the student never loses work. - **Observed core promise:** Repairs, notes, highlights, and parser reruns never rewrite imported source text or erase its representations. - **Observed user feeling:** “I can add my learning without corrupting the source.” - **Observed product truth:** Annotations are learner-owned overlays; provenance is source evidence. Observable evidence (Q-06): Highlights preserve exact source text; source corrections reconcile or mark anchors for repair; manual repair does not rewrite attempts or source excerpts. Observed evidence (H-21): implemented and verified. Evidence summary (H-21): Highlights/notes survive learning activity and source corrections without mutating imported question text. Ambiguous anchors are repairable, never guessed. Observable evidence (H-21): source hash plus offsets/context, repair status, keyboard/non-color semantics, reload/backup persistence, no device preference leak.

## CAND-000107 — Field-level provenance and inspectable parser evidence

## CAND-000109 — Batch intake and exception handoff

- **Observed Area/System:** Import
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-09): Implemented, not independently rerun in this reconstruction. Evidence summary (Q-10): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve a useful source even when it cannot yet produce valid Questions. - **Observed product principle:** Sources are never lost; truthful partial capability beats fabricated output. - **Observed core promise:** A source can be saved without fake Questions and parsed locally later. - **Observed user feeling:** “My file is safe even when AXOM cannot yet interpret it.” - **Observed product truth:** A Source Document is not a Question, and an empty parse is not permission to invent records. Observable evidence (Q-10): No-question sources save library-only; failed validation creates no empty set; saved document identity is reused when Questions are created later; local parsing requires no AI provider.

## CAND-000111 — Layered current parser behavior

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Question parsing
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-11): Implemented and regression-covered for the shipped parser; not equivalent to the planned ten-stage Universal Engine. Evidence summary (Q-11): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve document structure well enough to separate questions, options, answers, explanations, and metadata. - **Observed product principle:** Deterministic before AI. - **Observed core promise:** Structure is parsed without swallowing the next question or treating ordinary A–E prose as options. - **Observed user feeling:** “What I imported still looks like the question I own.” - **Observed product truth:** Question boundaries require structural evidence, not keywords alone. Observable evidence (Q-11): Numbered and unnumbered forms, Q/Question prefixes, multiline stems/options, OCR-like spacing, inline options, trailing keys, later explanations, and A–E-leading rationale prose have focused tests.

## CAND-000113 — Exception-based mapping inspection and repair

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Import review / Question detail
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-13): Partial: a review-gated current surface exists; the Universal Engine's full exception queue is planned. Evidence summary (Q-13): - **Observed design intent:** Require deliberate review only where evidence cannot safely establish a runnable mapping. - **Observed product principle:** Review is the exception, not the default workflow. - **Observed core promise:** A learner can inspect evidence, confirm or repair a mapping, and never have attempts rewritten by that repair. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM shows me exactly what needs my judgment.” - **Observed product truth:** Review volume and false trust are different problems. Observable evidence (Q-13): Current review shows candidates and conflicts, supports deliberate confirmation, preserves non-answer review gates, and never advertises unresolved Questions as runnable.

## CAND-000114 — Structured answer evidence preserves harmless drift

- **Observed Category:** Bug; Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Pasted text / CSV / JSON answer mapping; Question Integrity; Cross-product/Question Bank; Question Import
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-14): Verified fixed across both shipped import paths. Evidence summary (Q-14): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve explicit source evidence without turning it into trusted truth when trailing wording drifts. - **Observed product principle:** Trust over cleverness. - **Observed core promise:** A valid explicit letter with harmless textual drift remains a review-gated candidate; an exact cross-option contradiction remains unresolved. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM does not lose the answer carrying it across the room.” - **Observed product truth:** Candidate is not equivalent to runnable. Observable evidence (Q-14): Exact/letter-only resolves; singular, parenthetical, or explanatory drift preserves candidate plus review gate; cross-option contradiction preserves both keys with no chosen answer; CSV and JSON behave like pasted text; mixed `B,D,A,C,E` stays exact. Observed evidence (H-06): implemented and verified for pasted text and CSV/JSON. Evidence summary (H-06): Product truth: a higher unresolved rate is preferable to a wrongly trusted answer. Explicit evidence is preserved; uncertainty is surfaced; no path defaults to A. Observable evidence (H-06): `falseReady = 0`, mixed keys do not collapse, harmless drift remains a review-gated candidate, contradiction remains unresolved, and user confirmation outranks reparsing. Observed evidence (H-27): Verified. Evidence summary (H-27): users can understand current product truth and trust imported questions through inspectable provenance. Observable evidence (H-27): mixed keys preserved, no fallback A, real-import verifier, source-only save, truthful onboarding/help/feedback, deterministic Command Brief. Observed evidence (H-29): Verified. Evidence summary (H-29): preserve explicit evidence without treating drift as trusted. Observable evidence (H-29): harmless drift candidate, exact contradiction unresolved, unknown never A, false-ready zero. Observed evidence (H-32): Verified. Evidence summary (H-32): evidence quality, not file format, governs answer trust. Observable evidence (H-32): resolved/candidate/conflict/unresolved result, user confirmation, non-runnable candidates, diagnostics preserved.
- **Evidence Sources:** EVID-CAND-000114-001 — sources/question-system.md:428 (Q-14) — owner attachment /Users/jd/.codex/attachments/3dbc3f68-5a54-4646-9c08-35dd09549f77/pasted-text.txt:39-102,162-271; EVID-CAND-000114-002 — sources/question-system.md:428 (Q-14) — `docs/EXPERIENCE-REFINEMENT-WAVE.md:675-721,831-844; EVID-CAND-000114-003 — sources/question-system.md:428 (Q-14) — `web/src/lib/questionAnswerTrust.test.ts:21-102; EVID-CAND-000114-004 — sources/question-system.md:428 (Q-14) — `web/src/lib/questionStructuredAnswerTrust.test.ts:15-105; EVID-CAND-000114-005 — sources/question-system.md:428 (Q-14) — commits `b94e6f1 and `cdfff38.; EVID-CAND-000114-006 — sources/historical-review.md:140 (H-06) — Wave 5.5D lines 88–268; EVID-CAND-000114-007 — sources/historical-review.md:140 (H-06) — structured trust attachment lines 39–116 and 162–272; EVID-CAND-000114-008 — sources/historical-review.md:140 (H-06) — commits `b94e6f1 and `cdfff38.; EVID-CAND-000114-009 — sources/historical-review.md:439 (H-27) — commit `5c35e77; EVID-CAND-000114-010 — sources/historical-review.md:439 (H-27) — accepted 657 tests and 4 browser journeys in commit body.; EVID-CAND-000114-011 — sources/historical-review.md:470 (H-29) — commit `b94e6f1.; EVID-CAND-000114-012 — sources/historical-review.md:504 (H-32) — structured trust attachment; EVID-CAND-000114-013 — sources/historical-review.md:504 (H-32) — commit `cdfff38.

## CAND-000115 — Explanation and feedback text never contaminates an option

- **Observed Category:** Bug
- **Observed Area/System:** Parser / Quiz feedback
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-15): Verified fixed for the recorded failure shapes. Evidence summary (Q-15): - **Observed design intent:** Keep options and teaching explanations cognitively and semantically distinct. - **Observed product principle:** Trustworthy structure before visual polish. - **Observed core promise:** “Correct Feedback,” “Incorrect Feedback,” explanation, objective, source, and rationale markers do not become answer-choice text. - **Observed user feeling:** “The quiz shows the question cleanly instead of dumping extraction debris.” - **Observed product truth:** Option E is never an explanation container. ## CAND-000123 — Question Set is a deterministic membership snapshot

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Question Sets; Question Bank
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-23): Verified and lexically canonical. Evidence summary (Q-23): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve a learner's exact chosen practice population across time. - **Observed product principle:** One source of truth; reproducibility over hidden reshuffling. - **Observed core promise:** Stored `questionIds` are authoritative membership and retain their order across launch, reload, and supported backup. - **Observed user feeling:** “The set I saved is the set I get.” - **Observed product truth:** Filters, ordering mode, and seed describe creation provenance; they do not rebuild membership at runtime. Observable evidence (Q-23): Later tag edits do not change membership; stored order is preserved; missing/deleted IDs are skipped without reordering survivors; no random call occurs in explicit-membership resolution. Observed evidence (H-20): implemented and verified. Evidence summary (H-20): A filtered set is a reproducible snapshot, not a live reconstruction. The learner can relaunch the same session order after reload/backup. Observable evidence (H-20): explicit `questionIds` preserve relative order, skip deleted IDs, never require backlinks, never call randomness; legacy backlink sets remain compatible. Observed evidence (H-35): Implemented Evidence summary (H-35): learner tags remain distinct from imported curriculum metadata, and saved filtered sets are reproducible snapshots. Observable evidence (H-35): normalized duplicate prevention, keyboard-operable filters, explicit membership/order/seed provenance.

## CAND-000124 — Filter-created sets launch from explicit membership

- **Observed Category:** Bug
- **Observed Area/System:** Question Sets / Quiz launch; Question Sets
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-24): Verified fixed. Evidence summary (Q-24): - **Observed design intent:** Make the stored set card and actual runnable pool describe the same Questions. - **Observed product principle:** One source of truth. - **Observed core promise:** Snapshot sets do not require legacy question backlinks and never silently launch as an empty pool. - **Observed user feeling:** “A set that says it contains Questions actually starts.” - **Observed product truth:** Explicit membership wins; the legacy backlink path is a compatibility fallback only. Observable evidence (Q-24): Non-empty launch, exact valid-ID length, stored order, missing-ID skip, repeatability, JSON persistence, backup round-trip, no `Math.random`, and legacy fallback are covered. Observed evidence (H-36): Verified fixed. Documented product evidence (H-36): filter-created sets held `questionIds` but the runner used legacy `question.setId`, producing “No questions match.” Observable evidence (H-36): explicit IDs authoritative, no shuffle or `Math.random`, missing IDs skipped, legacy fallback preserved, reload/backup order identical.

## CAND-000130 — Question performance and error-pattern insights

- **Observed Category:** Feature; Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Question analytics; Question Analytics
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-30): Partial. Evidence summary (Q-30): - **Observed design intent:** Help the learner understand both what is weak and why misses recur. - **Observed product principle:** More insight, fewer raw numbers. - **Observed core promise:** Accuracy, weakest categories, confidence mismatch, error patterns, pacing, and improvement use stored learner evidence. - **Observed user feeling:** “My practice history explains what to work on next.” - **Observed product truth:** Insights require explicit denominators and sufficient evidence. Observable evidence (Q-30): Category accuracy, latest-attempt mastery, weak topics, and error-pattern grouping are tested; tiny samples do not support overclaimed faculty-style conclusions. Observed evidence (H-07): owner-resolved and implemented. Evidence summary (H-07): Current mastery answers “How am I doing now?” using the latest scored attempt per active question; all-attempt accuracy remains a separately named historical metric. Observable evidence (H-07): no ambiguous “Accuracy” label where either semantic could apply; retries do not permanently masquerade as current mastery.

## CAND-000133 — Answer-choice elimination

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Quiz reading tools; Quiz Player
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-33): Verified. Evidence summary (Q-33): - **Observed design intent:** Support active test-taking reasoning without changing the submitted answer. - **Observed product principle:** Power without complexity. - **Observed core promise:** Pointer or Shift+letter toggles elimination; Reset clears only eliminations; reveal preserves both elimination and correctness. - **Observed user feeling:** “I can reason through distractors the way I do on exam software.” - **Observed product truth:** Elimination is transient reasoning state, not selection or durable annotation. Observable evidence (Q-33): Does not select, remains legible, survives reveal, coexists with correct/wrong state, resets between Questions, and ignores typing/interactive contexts. Observed evidence (H-30): Verified. Evidence summary (H-30): reduce friction while reading and reasoning without changing answer state or canonical workspace data. Observable evidence (H-30): strikeout/shortcuts/reset, focus/scroll on advance, device-only scale, session calculator, rationale prioritization.

## CAND-000137 — Rationale reading hierarchy

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Quiz feedback; Quiz Player
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-37): Verified for source-provided rationales. Evidence summary (Q-37): - **Observed design intent:** Prioritize the explanation a learner needs after a response without overwhelming the screen. - **Observed product principle:** One primary learning message; other detail is progressive disclosure. - **Observed core promise:** Correct rationale comes first; on a miss, selected-wrong rationale comes next; remaining distractors are collapsed. - **Observed user feeling:** “I immediately see why the answer is right and why my choice was wrong.” - **Observed product truth:** A correct response never receives a false “your answer is wrong” section. Observable evidence (Q-37): Correct/wrong/empty rationale ordering, collapse behavior, and 68ch explanation measure passed recorded browser proof. Observed evidence (H-30): Verified. Evidence summary (H-30): reduce friction while reading and reasoning without changing answer state or canonical workspace data. Observable evidence (H-30): strikeout/shortcuts/reset, focus/scroll on advance, device-only scale, session calculator, rationale prioritization.

## CAND-000140 — Resilient annotation anchoring and repair

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Question annotations; Question Annotations
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-40): Verified. Evidence summary (Q-40): - **Observed design intent:** Keep learner highlights attached through safe source revisions without guessing ambiguous text. - **Observed product principle:** Trust over cleverness. - **Observed core promise:** Exact offsets are accepted only when valid; exact text/context may reanchor uniquely; ambiguity becomes `needs-repair`. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM preserves my annotation or asks me to repair it; it never moves it somewhere random.” - **Observed product truth:** Repair state preserves the original excerpt and remains authoritative until repaired or deleted. ## CAND-000141 — Annotation overlap integrity

- **Observed Category:** Bug; Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Question annotations; Question Annotations
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-41): Verified fixed. Evidence summary (Q-41): - **Observed design intent:** Keep persisted annotation state identical to what can render. - **Observed product principle:** One source of truth; do not silently trim, merge, or guess selection intent. - **Observed core promise:** A new highlight cannot overlap an active or repair-state highlight on the same target; touching edges remain valid. - **Observed user feeling:** “Every saved highlight is visible and understandable.” - **Observed product truth:** Stem, explanation, and each option are independent overlap domains. Observable evidence (Q-41): Partial/nested overlap rejects, adjacent ranges pass, targets remain isolated, `needs-repair` blocks, deletion permits recreation, blank/collapsed selections ignore, duplicate IDs and rapid navigation do not duplicate. Observed evidence (H-33): Verified. Evidence summary (H-33): storage and rendering tell the same truth; annotations are deterministic rather than silently trimmed or merged. Observable evidence (H-33): overlap rejected per target, adjacent allowed, repair anchors block, whitespace ignored, deletion affects only one record.

## CAND-000142 — Question-note image attachments

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Question annotations / Local Vault / Backup; Question Annotations
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-42): Implemented and focused-test verified; full repository gate evidence in the ledger is narrower than the surrounding checkpoints. Evidence summary (Q-42): - **Observed design intent:** Let learners preserve screenshots and images alongside their Question notes without bloating serialized Workspace data. - **Observed product principle:** Local first; the student never loses work. - **Observed core promise:** Valid images remain durable, described, viewable, removable, and portable under explicit backup semantics. - **Observed user feeling:** “The image I needed for this Question stays with my note.” - **Observed product truth:** Attachment metadata belongs to the Question; binary bytes live separately in the Local Vault. Observable evidence (Q-42): PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF validation, decode probe, count/byte limits, deterministic multi-file behavior, exact blob round-trip, alt-text editing, lightbox Escape/focus return, missing-blob safe state, localStorage exclusion, backup payload round-trip, and orphan maintenance have tests. Observed evidence (H-34): Implemented Evidence summary (H-34): learner-owned image notes extend annotations without source mutation. Observable evidence (H-34): owner Q2b design required separate binary lifecycle, explicit backup semantics, MIME/size controls, cleanup, and object URL revocation.

## CAND-000143 — Portable attachment backup is explicit and fault-tolerant

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Question attachments / Backup
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-43): Verified for current Question-note images. Evidence summary (Q-43): - **Observed design intent:** Make portable media recovery truthful without hiding missing or corrupt bytes. - **Observed product principle:** The student never loses work; uncertainty is surfaced. - **Observed core promise:** Backup clearly includes supported attachment payloads, deduplicates deterministically, and warns rather than deleting metadata when bytes are unavailable. - **Observed user feeling:** “I know whether my images are actually in the backup.” - **Observed product truth:** Separately stored bytes are not silently assumed to be in every serialized Workspace export. Observable evidence (Q-43): Exact-byte export/import, malformed and oversized rejection, ownership checks, later-update conflict behavior, and missing payload reporting are tested.

## CAND-000144 — Question and attachment deletion integrity

- **Observed Category:** Feature
- **Observed Area/System:** Question library / Attachments / Historical sessions
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-44): Verified for current deletion paths. Evidence summary (Q-44): - **Observed design intent:** Remove only what the learner explicitly targets while preserving historical evidence and neighboring content. - **Observed product principle:** Everything understandable and reversible where possible; no silent loss. - **Observed core promise:** Deleting a Question unlinks set membership without deleting session history; deleting one annotation or blob affects only that target. - **Observed user feeling:** “Delete means exactly what the confirmation says.” - **Observed product truth:** Relationship cleanup and historical record deletion are different operations. Observable evidence (Q-44): Set unlink preserves relative membership, session history remains, targeted annotation/blob deletion does not disturb neighbors, and bounded orphan maintenance touches only unreachable blobs.

## CAND-000152 — Universal Question Import Engine

- **Observed Category:** Feature; Research
- **Observed Area/System:** Import; Question Import
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-52): Planned and blocked at Wave 6A preconditions; current shipped parser is a legacy/accepted path, not this complete engine. Evidence summary (Q-52): - **Observed design intent:** Accept an entire module's heterogeneous lawful sources and produce correct, provenanced, review-routed Questions. - **Observed product principle:** Deterministic before AI; sources and uncertainty are never lost. - **Observed core promise:** Ready Questions are immediately practiceable; everything uncertain is an exception, never false truth. - **Observed user feeling:** “I can drop in the material I have and trust what comes out.” - **Observed product truth:** The engine is a staged evidence pipeline, not one monolithic PDF parser. Observable evidence (Q-52): The binding success definition and measured targets require correct boundaries, answers, explanations, provenance, review routing, zero invented answers/conflicts/default-A/lost sources/ overwritten corrections. Observed evidence (H-65): Deferred. Evidence summary (H-65): retain grounded source evidence and reduce manual reconstruction across PDFs, slides, images, tables, and OCR. Observable evidence (H-65): page/span provenance, bounded binary lifecycle, review before trust, no source mutation or silent invented answer.

## CAND-000157 — Original imported source-binary retention

- **Observed Category:** Product Decision
- **Observed Area/System:** Source Library / Storage
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-57): Architecture direction approved; implementation explicitly blocked. Evidence summary (Q-57): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve optional originals without forcing large binary payloads into every Workspace or backup. - **Observed product principle:** Local first; user controls storage cost. - **Observed core promise:** Extracted text remains default; “keep original” is an explicit per-import choice with quota truth. - **Observed user feeling:** “I decide whether AXOM keeps the original file and whether it travels in a backup.” - **Observed product truth:** Source-binary bytes do not belong in serialized Workspace JSON. Observable evidence (Q-57): Approved direction requires a separate binary store, opt-in retention, explicit backup inclusion, and quota warning.

- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-59): Partial. ## CAND-000165 — Faculty-style Question mode

- **Observed Category:** Research
- **Observed Area/System:** Question analytics / Block building
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-65): Proposed; analyzer exists, filter does not. Evidence summary (Q-65): - **Observed design intent:** Let learners practice structural patterns associated with a faculty source when enough evidence supports a restrained analysis. - **Observed product principle:** Hedge conclusions and refuse small-sample overclaim. - **Observed core promise:** No style filter appears as reliable without sufficient grounded Questions. - **Observed user feeling:** “AXOM can surface patterns without pretending to read a professor's mind.” - **Observed product truth:** Analyzer output is a hedged structural observation, not an answer source. ## CAND-000167 — Complex PDF layout recovery

- **Observed Category:** Technical Debt
- **Observed Area/System:** Import extraction
- **Evidence Summary:** Observed evidence (Q-67): In progress / foundation required. Evidence summary (Q-67): - **Observed design intent:** Preserve reading order and structure in columns, tables, unusual positioning, and damaged text layers. - **Observed product principle:** Layout evidence is part of source truth. - **Observed core promise:** Digital extraction remains page-aware, and uncertain layouts require review instead of fake confidence. - **Observed user feeling:** “A difficult PDF fails honestly instead of becoming a plausible-looking wrong Question.” - **Observed product truth:** Plain extracted text is insufficient for every PDF. Observable evidence (Q-67): Normalized pages retain coordinates, typography, vectors/images, rendered page reference, and computed reading order where available.
- **Evidence Sources:** EVID-CAND-000167-001 — sources/question-system.md:1899 (Q-67) — `ROADMAP.md:42; EVID-CAND-000167-002 — sources/question-system.md:1899 (Q-67) — `docs/UNIVERSAL-QUESTION-IMPORT-ENGINE.md:105-119; EVID-CAND-000167-003 — sources/question-system.md:1899 (Q-67) — `docs/WAVE-6-CONSOLIDATION.md:40-53.
